Please try again.'); } } // DRAFT ANALYSIS function runDraftAnalysis() { const draft = document.getElementById('draft-input').value.trim(); const request = document.getElementById('draft-request').value.trim(); const agent = document.getElementById('draft-agent').value; const priority = document.getElementById('draft-priority').value; if (!draft) { showNotif('Paste a draft first'); return; } const dnaCtx = buildDNAContext(); const sys = `You are a professional music production AI assistant specializing in lyrics, hooks, and song structure. ${text.replace(/\n/g,'

`; STATE.lastOutput.draft = text; } function updateMetrics(data) { if (data.hook_score) document.getElementById('m-hook').textContent = data.hook_score + '%'; if (data.cadence_score) document.getElementById('m-cadence').textContent = data.cadence_score + '%'; if (data.lex_score) document.getElementById('m-lex').textContent = data.lex_score + '%'; if (data.structure) document.getElementById('m-struct').textContent = data.structure; if (data.dna_match) { document.getElementById('m-dna').textContent = data.dna_match; document.getElementById('m-dna').style.color = 'var(--success)'; } if (data.dna_match) { document.getElementById('dna-match').textContent = data.dna_match; const pct = parseInt(data.dna_match); document.getElementById('dna-bar').style.width = pct + '%'; } } // REVISION MODE function runRevision() { const current = document.getElementById('rev-current').value.trim(); const edit = document.getElementById('rev-edit').value.trim(); const protect = document.getElementById('rev-protect').value.trim(); const options = document.getElementById('rev-options-text').value.trim(); const agent = document.getElementById('rev-agent').value; const format = document.getElementById('rev-format').value; if (!current) { showNotif('Add your draft first'); return; } const dnaCtx = buildDNAContext(); const sys = `You are a world-class lyric revision specialist.
